No module in `scripts/` builds a `MarketOrder` today (grep clean), so this is latent, and the tripwire only requires a new caller to mention `check_order_limits`, not to carry a price. Fix: in the funnel, refuse a non-BAG order whose `lmtPrice` is not a positive number. |
| R-468 | P2 | `scripts/health_service/serve.py:158`, `:185-192`; `scripts/health_service/probes.py:157`, `:274-277` | The REL-135 dwell holds for the shipped claim (`899s -> degraded`, `901s -> down`, executed) but is in-process wall-clock memory that covers UNITS only: `_non_up_since` lives on the cache object (`serve.py:158`) stamped from `time.time()` (`:185`), so every `radon-health.service` restart resets a dead `radon-monitor` to `degraded` for another 900s; and `aggregate_state` reads `non_up_secs` only for names in `DEPENDENCY_UNITS` (`probes.py:274-277`), so the `ib-gateway` PROBE `down` with units up and the nested `auth_state=awaiting_2fa` both report `degraded` with no input that could ever escalate them. 857bbad0 does NOT bypass the dwell (`_local_aggregate_serving_path_ok` returned `False` on the post-dwell `down` payload). Bounded (15 min per health restart; the gateway has its own on-box watchdog). Fix: persist first-seen per name in the state file the daemon already writes, and stamp a dwell on dependency probes too. |

### Audited, clean

- `cloud/services/radon-ib-gateway.service`, `radon-relay.service`, `radon-monitor.service` — nothing
  in range restarts or signals the gateway on deploy: `unit_is_excluded` keeps it out of
  stop-clean/restart-managed/recover (`deploy-root-helper.sh:315-319`, `:1588-1607`); bootstrap and
  the privileged refresh only install the unit file + `daemon-reload`; `wait_for_gateway_ready` is a
  read-only `/health` poll (`deploy.sh:1195-1209`); `gateway_control` fires only from operator
  `radon stop|start|restart` and is now role-gated. Removing `Wants=`/`PartOf=` leaves boot start
  intact via `[Install] WantedBy=multi-user.target`.
- `cloud/scripts/deploy.sh` RECOVER_ONLY (2ed40283) — `flock -n 9` at `:1880` returns 75 when any
  other deploy or prestage owns the lock, so it cannot recover a transition another deploy is
  legitimately in.
- `cloud/scripts/radon-app-runtime.sh` `cmd_pull` interruption — `timeout 600` (`deploy.sh:392`) ->
  return 1 -> `main` aborts at `:1734` before `restart_services`; partial pulls leave the tier
  untouched; `rmi` failures are `|| true` and docker refuses to remove an image referenced by a
  running container. `render_env_file` output is root `0600` on tmpfs, rewritten per start, never
  mounted into the container (only the fail-open in R-438 remains).
- `cloud/scripts/radon-app-runtime.sh` notify proxy lifecycle — per-unit socket path (`:261`),
  unlinked before bind (`:226-228`, `:266`), proxy exits within 250 ms of the docker client dying
  (`:235-243`) and is inside the cgroup for `KillMode` sweeps; a proxy that dies while the container
  runs makes keepalives ECONNREFUSED and `WatchdogSec` restarts the unit — fail-loud, not
  healthy-forever.
- `cloud/docker-compose.yml` — `${IB_GATEWAY_TAILSCALE_BIND:-100.112.32.16}` is interpolated from
  `--env-file "$ENV_FILE"` at `ib-gateway-control.sh:217`, so the documented `/etc/radon/env` knob
  is live. `cloud/config/sudoers.d/radon-deploy` `pull [0-9a-f]*` is a glob, but `cmd_pull` refuses
  anything not 40-hex (`:142-145`).
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` deploy ordering — deploy/stage-release/prepull all carry `app-images`
  in `needs` and require `result == 'success'` (`:698/:704`, `:785/:790`, `:828/:833`); the reusable
  workflow completes only after both image jobs' single push op, so no deploy starts on an unpushed
  image; caller+callee `cancel-in-progress: true` turns a superseded build into `cancelled`, which
  `!cancelled()` blocks. Cloud `edge` shard (92278f6a): `compgen -G` expansion with exact omit and an
  empty-selection guard (`:485-499`).
- `scripts/ci/path_filter.py` — every money path arms its gate: anything under `scripts/` arms python
  and web; `web/app/api/orders/*` and `web/lib/useOrderRisk*` arm web and the enumerated pytest
  contracts; unknown trees and empty ranges force both gates (`:259-262`, `:281-283`);
  `test_every_python_test_that_reads_web_has_a_cross_tree_contract` derives readers by AST.
- `cloud/.gitleaks.toml` — the new entry is commit-scoped AND rule-scoped (`[[rules.allowlists]]
  commits` under `literal-tws-credential-assignment`); the fixture is now f-string built so no later
  SHA matches `\bTWS_(USERID|PASSWORD)`.
- `docker/app/Dockerfile.node` — build args are `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` only (`:64-68`); no secret ARG/ENV;
  root-owned immutable trees with radon-owned `.next/cache` + `public/data` (`:84-87`).
- `cloud/caddy/Caddyfile` — `/api/assistant*` has no retry loop (POST, no idempotency key);
  catch-all `lb_retry_match GET HEAD` still covers `/api/orders/*`; all reverse_proxy dials are
  `127.0.0.1`; `/ws-headlines` is proxied to `127.0.0.1:8766` before the `/ws*` catch-all with
  forwarding headers that make the hub require a ticket.
- `scripts/watchdog/external_probe.py` (7a45f294, 857bbad0) — NF-10 answers: dwell is keyed on the
  deploy transition journal (`:31-33`, suppression only while the journal is < 3600 s old,
  `:202-206`) and the green marker (`[marker-900, marker+60]`, `:211-213`); a deploy that dies and
  leaves the journal is suppressed for at most 60 min; the suppression at `:289-308` runs after the
  `aggregate_down` local-clear and before the stranded-journal alarm; both branches still require
  `_local_aggregate_serving_path_ok()` at check time (`:81-98`), and `dependency_stuck` past 900 s
  collapses to `down` (`probes.py:273-297`). `scripts/health_probe/probe.py:512-522` emits
  `status_unreachable:caddy` only for the exact Caddy floor body, after the 2xx check.
- `cloud/scripts/db_backup.py` B2 retry (87233bf1) — `call_s3_with_retry` retries whole calls up to
  `S3_MAX_ATTEMPTS=3` with a fixed 1.0 s sleep, only for the named transient classes; exhaustion
  propagates to `state="error"`, `write_service_health("error")` and exit 1 (`:797-808`) — not silent.
- `scripts/knowledge/sources/incidents.py` (f0a4a3a1) — the skip key IS content-derived (store hashes
  source/scope/doc_key/title/summary/content/metadata, `schema.py:17-30`), so a changed incident
  always re-distills.
- `scripts/setup_reliability_weekend.sh`, `scripts/setup_testing_weekend.sh` (14065b74) — the
  `web/.env` copy is `install -m 600`, matches `.gitignore:82`, and both wrappers exclude it from
  `git clean`; provisioning is skipped when run from the runner clone. `scripts/dev` adds
  `headlines` with no kill-others, so a hub crash does not take the others down.
- `scripts/fetch_dispersion.py` — client id `connect(client_id="auto")` allocates from
  `SUBPROCESS_ID_RANGE = (20, 49)` with rotation on "already in use" (`ib_client.py:409-437`), no
  collision with fixed daemon ids; `try/finally: client.disconnect()` (`:209-212`); every
  `reqHistoricalDataAsync` carries ib_insync's `timeout=45` plus an outer `wait_for(50)`;
  `TimeoutStartSec=900`; the weekend path makes no network calls and heartbeats `ok` with
  `source: stored`; `_serve_stale` writes `error` with class `stale_source` and exits 1; the writer
  dedupes, chunks at 400 and is idempotent per date. `dispersion_math.py`: flat window raises
  `ValueError("zero variance")` (verified), trailing rolling mean, no forward fill.
- `cloud/services/radon-dispersion.{service,timer}`, `radon-model-catalog.{service,timer}` —
  byte-for-byte the fleet shape of `radon-vixts`; 22:20 UTC is post-close in EST and EDT; 03:10 UTC
  collides with nothing listed; both catalogued in both catalogs with matching names.
- `web/lib/assistant/catalog.ts` — `normalizePath` decodes up to 4 times, POSIX-normalizes, rejects
  `//`, `://`, `\`, NUL, drops the query before matching (`:165-183`); a "read" GET never unlocks a
  different verb; unknown paths default-deny; `classifyDenied` covers `/orders/{place,cancel,modify}`,
  `/trading*`, `/paper*`, `/admin*`, `/ib*`, `/api/admin*`, `/pi/exec` (`:189-213`). Verified in
  the lead: the model cannot place, modify or cancel an order, toggle the halt, restart a service, or
  reach admin through `call_api`. `dispatch.ts` caps the body at 8 KB, strips prototype keys, drops
  GET bodies, fences and truncates results at 24 k chars; `executeTool` still refuses `place_order`.
- `web/app/api/assistant/route.ts` streaming (11db0de9) — all 400/401/429 paths precede the stream;
  the `start` frame is written synchronously; a mid-turn throw becomes an `error` frame then
  `finish()`; `done` and `error` are in mutually exclusive arms. Image trust boundary: 4 images, 5 MB
  decoded each, PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP allowlist, base64 shape check, mirrored client-side; history is
  re-sent text-only. `web/lib/chat.ts`: a truncated stream becomes `TRUNCATED_STREAM_MESSAGE`, no
  automatic client retry.
- `web/lib/agent/turnSteps.ts` (0f33b878) — pre-fix runtime was last-key-wins with identical label
  text, so behaviour is unchanged; `agent-derivations.test.ts:80-81` pins both labels.
- `scripts/refresh_model_catalog.py` secrets — keys are placed only in request headers; `_log` prints
  urllib exception text, which does not echo headers; catalog rows carry only ids. `writer.py:1155-1194`
  single multi-row `ON CONFLICT(provider) DO UPDATE`, `if not rows: return` so an empty list never
  deletes. `web/app/api/models/route.ts`: Clerk-gated, `force-dynamic` + no-store, serializes only
  `{models, defaultId, source}`.
- `scripts/mktnews/protocol.js`, `normalize.js`, `format.js`, `store.js` — `parseFrame` catches JSON
  errors; frame size bounded by `maxPayload` (`client.js:77`) and again at `hub.js:84`, `:124`;
  `clip` bounds content to 2000 chars with a linear regex; no HTML produced; ring restore is
  `LIMIT ringSize`; prune is in the same write batch as the upsert; the writer is the HTTP transport
  with `boundedFetch` (10 s), not raw Hrana-over-WS. `hub.js` listener is forced to loopback
  (`:30-33`); ticket auth treats Caddy-forwarded loopback as untrusted (`wsTrust.js:39-43`, `:65-75`).
  `HeadlinesTape.tsx` renders the 【…】 lede as React text nodes inside `<strong>` — no
  `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`; `web/lib/headlinesSocket.ts` rejects any `mktnews.net` URL before the
  browser could dial upstream directly.
- `scripts/newsfeed/push_media.js`, `mediaPermissions.js` (0785f6f9) — in-place detection compares
  `realpathSync`; the container runs as the host radon uid/gid; media dir `0750 radon:radon` with
  `caddy` in group radon; files `0644` after write. A missing mount fails `fs.ensureDir` at boot and
  the unit restart-loops (operator-visible); a per-image write failure persists the post without the
  image, not with a 404 link.
- `web/lib/utils.ts` `formatSyncLine` — derives "Synced Nm ago (HH:MM ET)" from `last_sync`, says
  "Sync time unavailable" on an unparseable value; complies with the no-hardcoded-freshness rule.

## Delta audit 2026-08-31

**Range:** `fda36450..HEAD` (`39bf6f5e`) — 51 commits, 83 substantive source files after dropping tests,
docs, `site/`, JSON and images. Anchor verified (`git rev-parse --verify fda36450^{commit}` resolves to
`fda364508a9555e904eddcdb1adce1bf0d7bd1b0`; `git merge-base --is-ancestor fda36450 HEAD` confirms). The
range contains both weekend loops' own remediation merges (PR #188 `reliability/2026-08-30`, 13 commits;
PR #189 `testing/2026-08-30`, 1 commit) plus the testing loop's squash-merged second PR (#198, 7c627f30,
which touches money-path code: `exit_orders.py`, `journal_realized.py`, `cri_scan.py`, `positionUtils.ts`),
so per the 2026-08-30 lesson it was split by branch ancestry — `git log fda36450..HEAD ^f6cdee95^2
^ec04f800^2` gives the 37 feature commits — and 7c627f30 was handed to the executing regression walk
rather than a feature walk. Seven subsystem walks were scoped to the feature files, each capped at 10-14
files (Robinhood client, Robinhood rung in the ladders, Gateway remote control plane, FastAPI/admin side of
the split, STREAKS, nightly loop runners + CI filter, health suppression/feeds/assistant catalog), and two
EXECUTING walks reviewed the 2026-08-30 remediation (REL-153…REL-165) plus 7c627f30. Five loops were
running concurrently on this host (`launchctl list | grep radon` -> 5 labels, all in `cycle`), so no walk
ran a full suite.

**No branch collision.** `git ls-remote --heads origin 'reliability/*'` was run BEFORE numbering and
returned nothing (both prior nightly branches are merged and deleted). **Numbering starts at R-469:** the
max over both documents and `git log --grep` since the anchor is R-468 / REL-167 with no operator-consumed
ids this range. Backlog continues at REL-168.

**Standing sweeps** ran in the lead context before the walks reported. Sweeps 1-5 hold: halt chokepoints
at `ib_order_manage.py:183`, `ib_execute.py:444`, `exit_order_service.py:345`, `ib_place_order.py:242`,
`server.py:2762`, `handlers/exit_orders.py:477`; `_NON_IDEMPOTENT_IB_SCRIPTS` intact at `server.py:5269`
and enforced at `:5378`/`:5456`; `check_order_limits`/`check_quantity_limit` wired at `ib_execute.py:455`,
`ib_order_manage.py:275-277`, `exit_order_service.py:352`, `ib_place_order.py:255`,
`clients/ib_client.py:722-724`, `handlers/exit_orders.py:689`, `server.py:2748`; the exit-orders ack-poll
present (`_ack_perm_id:191`, `_confirm_placement:197`, used at `:717`); `upsert_daemon_state` still routes
through `_hrana_execute` (`writer.py:2276-2289`). **Sweep 6 found a gap the REL-145 tripwire cannot see:**
`IBClient.place_bracket_order` (`ib_client.py:774-805`) places three legs with neither guard, and the
tripwire is module-granular (R-479). **Sweep 7:** `test_watchdog_catalog_parity.py` 13 passed and
`test_rel141_catalog_exemptions_are_true.py` + `test_rel145_order_limits_at_every_funnel.py` 39 passed at
HEAD; no new timer landed this range (`git diff --name-only -- cloud/services/` shows only
`radon-ib-gateway-remote.service`, a socket-less daemon, not a scheduled job), and the two new Python jobs
(`fetch_rh_crowding.py`, unscheduled; `/streaks/{ticker}`, on-demand) write no `service_health` row by
design. **Manifest sweep:** 0 mismatches; the only unpinned units are `radon-llm-index.{service,timer}`
with `not-installed:` acks; `radon-mktnews.service` is now pinned and its ack removed.

**Regression review of REL-153…REL-165 and 7c627f30, by EXECUTION.** All eleven reviewed fixes HOLD on
their shipped tests run in isolation (dispersion 109+39+11, mktnews 7+6+5, assistant 7+5+4, flow-analysis
10+6+16, model-catalog 57, tagger 19+37, flex claim 31, db_backup 75, sync-control-plane 57 (ran on darwin),
T-313 funnel 14, journal_realized 49). Each walk also ran ONE case the shipped test does not cover, and four
of those surfaced defects filed as P2 — REL-153's Yahoo reserve is spent into a 429 (R-515), REL-164's flat
8h TTL makes a Friday EOD report stale all weekend (R-516), REL-160 has no local-retention valve under a
sustained B2 outage (R-517), and T-313's per-leg `result["error"]` is overwritten and the refused leg
re-refused every cycle (R-518). REL-156 was driven against real libsql with two claimants, a post-commit
mark failure, a stale lease and a clock step backwards; the stale-lease re-apply is idempotent on the
`cash_flows.id` fingerprint (no double-booking). The `journal_realized` P0 history (REL-094/109, R-320) was
re-executed: `strike=0`, `right=''`, `strike='0'` all refuse with the WARN; healthy -> 3000.0; a triple
cross-writer duplicate carries no false taint. Drills in isolation: `test_position_reconcile_spine` 21,
`test_trading_halt` 7, `test_order_limits` 33, `test_order_limits_combo_quadrants` 14,
`test_ib_execute_order_limits` 9, and in the lead `test_monitor_daemon/test_exit_orders_ack.py` and
`test_exit_orders_guard_durability.py` (the executing walk reported the ack drill as missing because it looked
in `scripts/tests/` rather than `scripts/tests/test_monitor_daemon/`; the lead ran it).

**Severity decisions in the lead.** Every P1 was verified by reading or executing in the lead before it
landed: the refresh-token order (R-469, read `:325` vs `:378`), the role-file split (R-472, grep of every
reader), the auth-bypass scope (R-473, `server.py:873` and `auth.py:121`), the 120s status timeout (R-474,
`REMOTE_TIMEOUT_S = GATEWAY_CONTROL_TIMEOUT_S`), the CI filter (R-476, `select_gates` executed on three
paths), the plist fetch (R-477, the `ProgramArguments` string), the set-exclusion (R-478, `probes.py:169`),
the bracket funnel (R-479, `place_bracket_order` body + tripwire regex). One walk rating was LOWERED: the
same-second launchd fire (R-503) was filed P1 by the loops walk and is P2 here because its blast radius is
the loops' own output and the runner host, not a production path. Two were filed with an explicit
contingency rather than raised: R-472 (the operator CLI and the app-role POST carve-out are the primary
gates; the hollow layer is defence-in-depth) and R-479 (no caller exists). The auth-bypass finding was
kept at P1, not P0, because the lead verified forwarding headers deny the bypass — the exposure is the
private-net members, not the internet.

**Cross-walk convergence.** Six findings were reached independently by two walks each and merged before
numbering: the Robinhood failure classifier (client + ladder -> R-470), the post-failure storm (client +
ladder -> R-480), the app-role guard (Gateway-remote + FastAPI -> R-472), the 2FA contract across the split
(FastAPI + Gateway-remote -> R-475), the non-monotonic deadlines (Gateway-remote + FastAPI -> R-499), the
CRI lock move (ladder + executing money walk -> R-487) and the headlines REST-fed status (health + executing
feeds walk -> R-513). One walk rating was contradicted by a lead verification and the row records it:
the FastAPI walk itself listed header spoofing as CLEAN after tracing Caddy and uvicorn, which is why
R-473 is scoped to private-net peers.

**The week's theme is the broker/app split.** Seven of the eleven P1s and eleven P2s are in the five PRs
that moved the IB Gateway to its own VM (#205-#209 plus the role hardening): a new stdlib daemon whose
accept thread does the TLS handshake, an auth-trust widening that was meant for `/health` and landed on
everything, a role guard reading a non-canonical env file, a 5s UI poll bound to a 120s blocking call, and a
2FA push-lock contract that the app side can no longer see. The second theme is the Robinhood rung: a new
vendor inserted into eight ladders in one commit, with the NF-6 classifier defect shipped on day one. NF-10
recurs (the weekend gateway suppression is unbounded), NF-3 recurs on the headlines tape, and NF-9 does not
recur (the two new jobs write no heartbeat by design).

### Findings

| ID | Sev | Where | Finding |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-469 | P1 | `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:323-379`, `:255-267` | **The single-use Robinhood refresh token is spent before anything proves it can be persisted.** `_refresh_locked` POSTs `grant_type=refresh_token` at `:325` and only then calls `persist()` at `:378`; when the write fails (ENOSPC, `os.replace` EPERM, the token dir unwritable while `<file>.lock` already exists) the rotated `refresh_token` exists only in this process and disk keeps the spent one. Verified in the lead by reading the path: endpoint spends RT1, disk still RT1, memory RT2, `_refresh_disabled` False, so the NEXT process gets `invalid_grant`, `_disable_for_process` fires, and every scheduled ladder runs on Yahoo (rule 7) until an operator re-authorizes. Walk: RH client. |
| R-470 | P1 | `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:743-755`, `:486-489`, `:520-524`, `:461-464`, `:490-520`, `:134-137`, `:349-355`; `scripts/portfolio_risk.py:543-548`; `scripts/rv_ratio_scan.py:336-339`; `scripts/fetch_credit_spread.py:322-327`, `:541-546`; `scripts/fetch_iei_hyg.py:304`, `:477`; `scripts/garch_convergence.py:205-208`; `scripts/leap_scanner_uw.py:307-310`; `scripts/cri_scan.py:693-703` | **NF-6 on the new Robinhood rung of eight ladders: auth, rate-limit and network failures are indistinguishable from "no data" and demote silently to Yahoo under an `ok` heartbeat.** `fetch_robinhood_closes` catches `RobinhoodClientError` (`:747`) and bare `Exception` (`:753`) and returns `{}` for 401/403 (`:520`), 429/5xx (`:521-524`) and connect/timeout (`:486-489`) alike; every caller treats `{}` as "no data" and falls to Yahoo, so credit-spread/IEI-HYG heartbeat `ok` with `source="yahoo"`/`"ib+yahoo"` (`:541-546`) and portfolio_risk/rv persist `source="yahoo"` rows with no auth/rate-limit class anywhere in `service_health`. Two aggravations from the client walk: (a) with an access-token-only store (`ROBINHOOD_MCP_TOKEN` alone, allowed by `.env.example:317`) both `_disable_for_process` branches sit behind `can_refresh()` / `_retry_auth=False`, so `robinhood_configured()` stays True and every symbol in every ladder re-sends `initialize` and eats a 401 for the rest of the process and all later processes (executed: 3 POSTs for 3 symbols, still configured); (b) `_disable_for_process` leaves only a stderr print, no `service_health` row or alert in either the client or `fetch_rh_crowding.py`. Also an unwrapped requests error (`ChunkedEncodingError`, `TooManyRedirects`) on symbol 1 aborts the whole batch at `:753` instead of per-symbol at `:747`. R-296's UW-leg class, now on the RH leg; reached independently by the RH-client and RH-ladder walks. |
| R-471 | P1 | `scripts/ib_gateway_remote/serve.py:253-255`, `:20`, `:239-240`, `:188-193` | **One half-open TCP connection freezes the broker's Gateway-control daemon, `/healthz` included.** `make_server` wraps the LISTENING socket (`httpd.socket = ctx.wrap_socket(...)`, `:255`), so the TLS handshake runs synchronously in `get_request` on the `serve_forever` thread with no socket timeout, and the IP allowlist (`:190`) is checked only AFTER the handshake. Any host on 10.0.0.0/16 that connects and never sends ClientHello blocks every later request until it closes. Reproduced by the walk: a concurrent mTLS `GET /healthz` -> `TimeoutError: handshake operation timed out`, recovers on close. The app's `show_unit` then waits its full 120s on every poll (see the blocking-poll finding). Walk: Gateway remote. |
| R-472 | P1 | `cloud/scripts/ib-gateway-control.sh:23`, `:413-427`, `:429-435`; `cloud/scripts/deploy-root-helper.sh:240-246`; `cloud/scripts/operator-radon.sh:275-279`; `cloud/scripts/radon-app-runtime.sh:31`; `cloud/scripts/setup-vps.sh:21`; `scripts/api/services.py:109-114`; `scripts/api/server.py:2051-2056`, `:2098`, `:758`; `scripts/api/ib_gateway.py:1355-1363`, `:1427`, `:692`; `cloud/scripts/check-env.py:88-118` | **The app-role guard that stops the APP host from bringing up a second Gateway session has two holes.** (a) The helper's own `refuse_app_role_mutation` resolves `RADON_HOST_ROLE` from `ENV_FILE` = `/home/radon/radon-cloud/.env` (`:23`) while every other role reader — root helper, operator CLI, app runtime, setup-vps — canonicalizes `/etc/radon/env` (verified in the lead by grep); with the role set only in the canonical file and the helper reinstalled on the app VM (the exact 75cfffd8 incident, or the pre-ready compatibility deploy path) the guard evaluates `combined` and `start`/`restart` reaches compose. (b) FastAPI's default role is `combined` (`services.py:109-114`) and nothing in `restart_ib_gateway` or startup `ensure_ib_gateway()` is role-gated at runtime — the consistency check lives only in deploy-time `check-env.py` — so an app-host FastAPI whose env drifts (role line lost, `IB_GATEWAY_MODE != cloud`) takes the combined path to local `docker compose up -d`/`restart` (`ib_gateway.py:692`, `:751`), kicking the broker login and firing a push. Contingency: the operator CLI and the app-role POST carve-out (`server.py:2025-2042`) read the canonical file and are the primary gates today; this is the defence-in-depth layer being hollow. Gateway-remote and FastAPI walks converged. |
| R-473 | P1 | `scripts/api/auth.py:28`, `:48`; `scripts/api/server.py:873`, `:2025-2042`, `:847` | **The 10.0.0.0/16 trust added for the broker watchdog's `/health` probe unlocks the GLOBAL auth bypass, not `/health`.** `/health` was already in `AUTH_EXEMPT_PATHS` (`server.py:847`); `is_local_or_tailnet` now returns True for the Hetzner private net (`auth.py:48`) and feeds `is_trusted_local_request`, which is the whole-app bypass at `server.py:873`, so any peer on the private net (the broker VM, or any future VM attached to the network) can POST `/orders/place`, `/orders/cancel`, `/admin/stack/restart`, `/pi/exec` with no Clerk JWT, and on a `combined`/`broker`-role FastAPI even `/ib/restart` (the `_is_app_role_gateway_mutation` carve-out at `:2032` fires only when the role is `app`). Verified in the lead: the trust is on the TCP peer and forwarding headers deny the bypass, so an INTERNET client cannot spoof into it (Caddy sets `X-Forwarded-For`, uvicorn `--forwarded-allow-ips 127.0.0.1`); the exposure is exactly the private-net members, which is why this is P1 and not P0. The commit message names `/health` as the intended scope. Walk: FastAPI/admin. |
| R-474 | P1 | `scripts/api/services.py:309-320`, `:414-415`, `:455-463`, `:483`; `web/components/admin/AdminWorkspace.tsx:44` (`SERVICES_POLL_MS = 5_000`); `web/app/api/admin/services/route.ts:20` | **On the app host every 5s `/admin/services` poll makes a blocking 120s mTLS call to the broker.** `show_unit(GATEWAY_UNIT)` on role `app` calls `remote_gateway_action("status")` -> `asyncio.to_thread(_remote_http, action, REMOTE_TIMEOUT_S)` with `REMOTE_TIMEOUT_S = GATEWAY_CONTROL_TIMEOUT_S` = 120s (verified in the lead). A silently unreachable broker (VM down, SYN dropped, or the handshake freeze above) leaves each call hung ~120s while the Next route gives up at 15s and re-polls, so ~24 threads pile onto the default executor (`min(32, cpu+4)`, 6-8 on the VPS) and starve every other `to_thread` user — `restart_backoff_state`, `_check_2fa_push_lock_bounded`, `_do_probe` — which then time out and report synthetic "lease held"/degraded. Walk: FastAPI/admin. |
| R-475 | P1 | `scripts/api/ib_gateway.py:1268-1272`, `:275-290`; `scripts/api/services.py:322-327`, `:479-489`; `scripts/api/server.py:1874-1920`, `:2114-2121`; `web/components/admin/Ib2faControls.tsx:77-78`, `:109-128`; `web/lib/adminFormat.ts:275-281`; `scripts/ib_gateway_remote/serve.py:30`; `cloud/scripts/ib-gateway-control.sh:490-501`, `:535-539` | **The 2FA-aware restart contract (`scripts/api/CLAUDE.md`) does not survive the broker/app split.** On the app host `/health.ib_gateway.restart_backoff.push_lock` is read from the APP's own lock file, which the broker helper never holds, so `isForcePushDisabled` / `startBlockedByPush` are always false and "Force 2FA Push" and "Start Gateway" stay ARMED while a broker push is in flight; nothing proxies the broker's lease state into `/health`. `show_unit` translates only `running`/`stopped` (`services.py:322-327`), so the broker's `transition-pending` leaves `active_state="unknown"`, `gatewayPowerState` falls back to `portListening` (false mid-restart), and the panel reads "stopped" and arms Start during the very transition the "Gateway is mid-transition. Wait for it to settle." guard was written for. `/ib/reset-backoff` silently sends `reset-lease` to the broker (releasing its lease) under UI copy that says it clears the backoff counter, and on the broker side `reset-lease` and `stop` (which releases any lease unconditionally at `:500`) are both in `MUTATIONS` with no per-verb cooldown, so `Reset backoff` -> `Force 2FA`, or `Stop` -> `Start` within seconds fires a second IBKR push while the first is in flight. The only thing preventing a stacked push today is the broker's 409 on a HELD lease. FastAPI and Gateway-remote walks converged. |
| R-476 | P1 | `scripts/ci/path_filter.py:271-274`, `:285-286`, `:302-303`; `scripts/tests/test_docs_contract.py:129-133`, `:326`; `scripts/tests/test_security_loop_contract.py:94`; `scripts/tests/test_documentation_loop_contract.py:90`; `cloud/tests/test_integration.py:376`, `:412`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml:853-863` | **The new CI path filter skips BOTH test gates for every `.md`, but pytest asserts on `CLAUDE.md`, `scripts/CLAUDE.md`, `scripts/health_service/CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md` and the five loops' `SKILL.md` rails.** Executed in the lead: `select_gates(["CLAUDE.md"])`, `select_gates([".claude/skills/security-nightly/SKILL.md"])` and `select_gates(["scripts/CLAUDE.md"])` all return `python=False web=False contract_tests=()`. The deploy job accepts `skipped` for every gate (`ci.yml:853-863`), so a push that removes a rail from an unattended agent's SKILL.md, or edits a CLAUDE.md contract a test pins, skips the gate that would fail, deploys, becomes the new green base, and runs at 00:00 untested. Distinct from the testing loop's T-343, which covers only the dead `web/README.md` contract. Walk: nightly loops. |
| R-477 | P1 | `config/com.radon.security-daily.plist:18`; `config/com.radon.ci-performance-daily.plist:18`; `config/com.radon.documentation-daily.plist:18` (inherited from `config/com.radon.reliability-daily.plist:18`) | **All five loop plists run an unbounded `git fetch` BEFORE the wrapper's lock, traps and dead-man.** The `ProgramArguments` string is `kill -0 <pid> \|\| { git -C "$C" fetch -q origin && git checkout ... && git reset --hard ... }; exec ... cycle` (verified in the lead) with no `timeout`, no `ConnectTimeout`/`ServerAliveInterval` — the wrappers themselves bound every network call (`security_nightly.sh:92-97`, `:329`) but this hop is outside them. The R-237 port-22 blackhole therefore hangs the launchd job indefinitely with no lock held, no issue comment and no page, and launchd's single-instance-per-label silently drops every following 00:00 fire until the TCP session dies — the silent-dead class the wrapper fixes were meant to close, re-opened one layer up. Walk: nightly loops. |
| R-478 | P1 | `scripts/health_service/probes.py:169`, `:229-236`, `:281-284`, `:313`; `scripts/health_service/serve.py:158`, `:185-192`; `docs/incident-runbook.md` (8a0b95ce hunk); RELIABILITY_AUDIT.md REL-167 row | **NF-10 — the "weekend" IB-gateway suppression is an unconditional set-exclusion with no calendar, no dwell bound and no result discriminator.** 8a0b95ce defines `DWELL_ESCALATE_UNITS = DEPENDENCY_UNITS - {"radon-ib-gateway.service"}` (`:169`, verified in the lead), so a gateway unit dead at Tuesday 10:00 ET with `Result=exit-code` stays `degraded` forever and the off-box observer can never page `aggregate_down` for it; the runbook's "market closed + `Result=success` + `ConnectionRefusedError`" rule exists only in prose. The same commit inverts REL-167 ("stamp a dwell on the `ib-gateway` probe and `awaiting_2fa`") without touching the backlog row: `_non_up_since` is still in-process, and now neither the `ib-gateway` probe `down`, the unit `down`, nor nested `awaiting_2fa` has ANY escalation input, so REL-167 as written would ship a test the new exclusion makes unreachable. Contingency stated in the code comment: the on-box watchdog still pages `ib-gateway-grouped`; the edge floor is the layer that is now blind. Walk: health/suppression. |
| R-479 | P1 | `scripts/clients/ib_client.py:774-805` (`place_bracket_order`, three `self._ib.placeOrder` calls at `:795`); `scripts/tests/test_rel145_order_limits_at_every_funnel.py:358-378` | **`IBClient.place_bracket_order` places three live orders with neither `is_trading_halted()` nor `check_order_limits`/`check_quantity_limit`, and the REL-145 tripwire cannot see it.** Standing sweep 6 in the lead: the method (`:774-805`, present since 2026-03-04) calls `self._ib.placeOrder` per bracket leg with no guard, while `place_order` (`:700-735`) carries both. The tripwire passes because it is MODULE-granular — `test_every_place_order_call_site_references_the_limits` asserts only that `"check_order_limits" in body` for any file matching `\.placeOrder\(` (`:358-378`), which `ib_client.py` satisfies through `place_order`. Contingency: `grep -rn place_bracket_order` finds no caller outside `scripts/tests/test_ib_client.py`, so this is a latent funnel gap plus a test-scope defect (R-277/R-325/R-412 class: a fix shipped as "a test now enforces this" whose scope is narrower than the claim), not a live path — hence P1, not P0. |
| R-480 | P2 | `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:449-467`, `:480-520`, `:521-524`, `:559-573`, `:316-321`, `:336`, `:356-358`; `scripts/garch_convergence.py:196`, `:346-347`; `scripts/leap_scanner_uw.py:294`, `:632` | **Nothing circuit-breaks the Robinhood rung after a failure, so a dead or rate-limited MCP is hammered once per symbol per scan.** `_refresh_disabled` is consulted only in `_ensure_initialized`/`refresh`, not in `_resolve_token`/`_post`, so once `initialize` has succeeded and the MCP starts 401-ing each remaining symbol still POSTs the rejected token; a 429 is a generic `RobinhoodClientError` with no backoff and no disable; garch/leap call `fetch_robinhood_closes([ticker])` once per ticker from a 16-worker pool, each constructing a fresh client that redoes `initialize` + `notifications/initialized` (`:559-573`), so a rate-limited endpoint receives ~3 POSTs per ticker (~1500 for a 500-name universe when IB and UW are both empty). The refresh flock is held across the token-endpoint POST and a 5xx/timeout there does not disable, so 16 workers with stale tokens serialize ((N-1)×timeout ≈ 105s) and a token-endpoint outage costs one hit per symbol. RH-client and RH-ladder walks converged. |
| R-481 | P2 | `scripts/cri_scan.py:693`, `:508`, `:604-606`, `:1752-1757`; `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:77`, `:743`, `:766`; `scripts/data_refresh.py:36-41` | **The RH rung's worst-case cost overruns the CRI scan budget on a blackholed endpoint.** Each ladder call costs 3-4 POSTs × `LADDER_TIMEOUT_S=7` (~21-28s) with no circuit-break on timeout, so a CRI run on the post-close-snapshot path pays SPY history (~28s) plus VIX, VVIX and SPY quotes (~21s each) ≈ 90s on top of the documented 60-103s IB path, overrunning `CRI_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECS=180` and soft-failing to a stale `cri.json` for the duration of the outage. The client's own "4×7s = 28s" per-rung budget claim (`:77`) does not hold under the serialized refresh lock above. Walk: RH ladder. |
| R-482 | P2 | `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:325-337`, `:483-485`, `:529-539`, `:346` | **Both Robinhood outbound calls have only a per-read timeout, no total deadline and no body cap.** `requests.post(..., timeout=N)` bounds each socket read; `resp.text`/`resp.json()` read the whole body. A Streamable-HTTP server that holds the SSE response open with keepalive pings under 7s apart never trips `requests.Timeout`, so the rung hangs a ladder worker indefinitely. Walk: RH client. |
| R-483 | P2 | `scripts/fetch_rh_crowding.py:160-161`, `:179-188`, `:191-210`; `.env.example:313-314` | **`fetch_rh_crowding.py` crashes where its own docs promise a clean skip.** `rh.get_popular_watchlists()` and `persist()` sit outside any `try`, so `RobinhoodAuthError` (invalid_grant), an MCP 5xx `RobinhoodClientError`, or an `OSError` from the token-file write exit with a traceback and non-zero status, contradicting `.env.example:313-314` and the module docstring; no `service_health` row either way. Nothing schedules the job today (verified: no timer, plist or wrapper references it), so the blast radius is operator runs. Walk: RH client. |
| R-484 | P2 | `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:383-391`, `:148-152`, `:160-170`; `scripts/garch_convergence.py:196`; `scripts/leap_scanner_uw.py:294`; `scripts/cri_scan.py:400` | **`robinhood_configured()` re-reads the token file and re-prints the permissions warning on every call, and garch/leap call it once per ticker from 16 threads.** A 0644 `data/rh_mcp_token.json` emits roughly one interleaved stderr line per ticker per scan; the check is networkless (clean) but per ticker, not once. Walk: RH ladder. |

### Audited, clean

- `scripts/api/auth.py:109-126`, `cloud/caddy/Caddyfile`, `cloud/services/radon-api.service:44` — the
  private-net trust is on the TCP peer; any forwarding header denies the bypass; uvicorn runs with
  `--proxy-headers --forwarded-allow-ips 127.0.0.1` so Caddy-proxied traffic carries the real public IP. An
  internet client cannot spoof into R-473 (covered by `test_auth.py:203-206`).
- `scripts/ib_gateway_remote/serve.py:69-115`, `:118-125`, `:128-145`, `:195-217` — bind restricted to
  loopback or 10.0.0.0/16 with `0.0.0.0`/`::` refused; allowlist must be host addresses; absolute helper
  path; certs must exist or the process exits 2; `CERT_REQUIRED`, CA pinned, TLS 1.2 floor, no plaintext
  listener; fixed argv, `shell=False`, verb allowlisted twice, 120s helper timeout; forwarded headers ignored.
- `cloud/scripts/ib-gateway-control.sh:503-519`, `:437-450`, `:371-394`, `:452-462` — `start` is
  idempotent (running -> exit 0, no lease, never `down`), so 08bdd41b's per-unit start cannot become a
  restart; remote `restart` goes through the same durable 10m lease (rc 75 -> 409 -> `PUSH_LOCK_HELD_RC`),
  deploy lock and lifecycle mutex.
- `cloud/config/installed-units.sha256:98`, `cloud/scripts/setup-vps.sh:637-638`,
  `deploy-root-helper.sh:269-271` — "pinned, not enabled" is consistent across manifest, setup-vps (copies,
  does not enable) and role-`app` strip.
- `scripts/api/services.py:417-452`, `:618-626`, `:479-489`; `server.py:2025-2042` — remote URL must be
  https on loopback/10.0.0.0/16; missing CA/cert/key fails closed with no fallback to the helper or docker;
  409 / rc 74 / rc 75 map to HTTP 409; app-role Gateway POSTs lose loopback trust and need a Clerk JWT.
- `web/app/api/admin/ib/*`, `services/[unit]/[action]/route.ts` with `routeAccess.ts:122-129` —
  `operatorOnly` fail-closed, Clerk token forwarded as Bearer, unit regex and action allowlist mirror
  `services.py`; `AdminWorkspace.tsx:269-413` `useCallback` dep arrays complete; Force 2FA, Start, Stop and
  stack restart each have a wire test (`admin-action-request-assertions.test.tsx:141-316`).
- `scripts/clients/robinhood_client.py:158-186`, `:255-267`, `:277-291`, `:292-321`, `:343-358`,
  `:576-584`, `:656-660` — unconfigured path costs no network; persist is tmp + 0600 + `os.replace` with the
  lock fd closed in `finally`; single-flight refresh re-reads under the lock; permanent 4xx disables after one
  hit; `_scrub` runs before every truncation and covers both tokens; read-only allowlist is deny-by-default;
  `.gitignore:105` covers `data/rh_mcp_token.json*`.
- Ladder ordering (46897eec) — IB > UW > (Cboe) > RH > Yahoo in all eight ladders; index symbols skip RH;
  rv/portfolio trim bars after `last_completed_session_date()` before persisting `source="rh"`; the
  writer vocabulary includes `rh`; 7c627f30's `cri_scan.py` change adds a missing `import os` (a `NameError`
  every run hit at the lock) — behavioural, not test-only.
- `scripts/db/writer.py:960-1004`, `scripts/fetch_rh_crowding.py:179-188`, migration 0066 — chunked
  400-row upserts with one `recorded_at` per run; Turso first, JSON via tmp + `os.replace` only on success;
  idempotent DDL; nothing schedules the job and no doc claims otherwise.
- STREAKS: `streaks.py:43`, `:55-59`, `:210-212`; `price_cache.py:35`, `:43-44`, `:131-141`;
  `web/app/api/streaks/route.ts:16-55`; `useStreaks.ts:30-65` — ticker regex + 400, sha256 cache key (no
  traversal), empty result never cached, atomic cache write with quarantine on corrupt read, per-symbol
  single-flight, per-user rate limit, fail-closed 502 never `[]`, no polling, `source` surfaced end-to-end,
  no raw hex / em dashes / hardcoded cadence copy.
- `scripts/security_nightly.sh` (and by diff `ci_performance_nightly.sh`, `documentation_nightly.sh`) —
  inherited every prior wrapper fix: mkdir lock with owner-only release (R-411), `net_bounded` + ssh
  keepalive on every `gh`/`git fetch`, traps armed before the prologue (R-239/R-384), `timeout` without
  `--foreground` (R-386), fetch retry (R-237), launchd sinks excluded from rotation (R-267), single-parse
  `main`; wall-clock ≈ 8.3h < 24h; plist env carries no token; `ci.yml`'s 7c627f30 hunk removes no
  `needs:` and adds no `continue-on-error`; `path_filter.py` fails closed when the base cannot be resolved.
- `scripts/health_service/probes.py:281-284`, `:306-318`, `:349-350` — dwell still escalates
  `radon-newsfeed`/`radon-monitor`; serving-path `down`/`starting` ordered ahead of dependency suppression
  (R-398 holds); raw `units`/`probes` remain in the payload.
- `scripts/mktnews/hub.js:342-360`, `:156`, `:378-383`; `client.js:13`, `:36`, `:42`; `store.js:4-6` —
  flash poll gated on `upstreamState==="down"` or >5m silence, `pollInFlight`, stops when the WS frames
  again, `onlyNew` id-dedupe, 5s `AbortSignal.timeout`, `ON CONFLICT(id)` upsert, timers unref'd; one REST
  call per minute. `drift-allowlist.conf`: the `radon-mktnews.service` ack was removed as the unit was pinned.
- `web/lib/assistant/catalog.ts:142-164`, `:183-200`; `catalogBuild.ts:24-44`, `:67-71`;

## Delta audit 2026-09-02

**Range:** `39bf6f5e..HEAD` (`db25990d`) — 32 commits, ~97 substantive feature-side source files after
dropping tests, docs, `site/` and `context/`. Anchor verified (`git rev-parse --verify 39bf6f5e^{commit}`
resolves to `39bf6f5ecf661c87a566bc5707b4756848153609`; `git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms). The range
holds six loop-output squashes (Reliability #212, Testing #213, Documentation #210/#223, CI-Performance
#211/#217); the file sets were split per the standing lesson — eight subsystem walks capped at 8-14 files
over the 26 feature commits (credential store, setup wizard, hosted MCP, ma-ratio, nightly wrappers,
watchdog/fetchers, realtime/orders UI, CI/control plane) and one EXECUTING regression walk over the
25 loop-only files (REL-168…REL-174, REL-179, REL-180 + the testing squash). All nine finished in 3-13
minutes, none lost to the stream watchdog.

**The 2026-09-01 cycle did not run.** Rolling-issue forensics (issue #81, comments 07:19Z): every
remediate round died in seconds on 'You're out of usage credits' — the claude.ai subscription was
exhausted, all 8 rounds burned in ~40s with no quota-aware backoff, and no 09-01 audit or branch exists.
Consequences recorded here: REL-175…REL-178 and REL-181…REL-186 from the 2026-08-31 backlog were never
started and roll into THIS cycle's remediate phase; the operator's 09-01/09-02 commits (983cc8b8,
4d8ede84, db25990d — subscription-only billing, exhausted-rung fallback) are this range's response, and
the wrappers walk audited them (R-530, R-533, R-536).

**No branch collision.** `git ls-remote --heads origin 'reliability/*'` was run BEFORE numbering and
returned nothing (only `testing/2026-09-01` and `testing/2026-09-02` exist). **Numbering starts at
R-519:** the max over both documents plus `git log --grep` since the anchor is R-518/REL-186 (the delta's
commit messages consume nothing above R-517/REL-180). Backlog continues at REL-187.

**Standing sweeps** ran in the lead before the walks reported; sweeps 1-5 hold: halt chokepoint
(`ib_place_order.py:240-242`), `_NON_IDEMPOTENT_IB_SCRIPTS` intact (`server.py:5343`, enforced `:5452`/
`:5530`), order limits wired (`ib_place_order.py:253-255`), exit-orders ack-poll present
(`handlers/exit_orders.py:198`), `upsert_daemon_state` on hrana (`writer.py:2333`). Sweep 6: no new
order-placing call site bypasses the funnel — the delta's only new order-touching code is UI posting to
the guarded `/api/orders/place` (`MobileOrderTicket.tsx:515`) and `mcp_hosted` registers no write tool
(walk-verified); `place_bracket_order` remains open as REL-186. Sweep 7: `test_watchdog_catalog_parity.py`
13 passed at HEAD; the new `radon-ma-ratio.timer` is registered in both catalogs
(`watchdog/services.py:176-180`, `serviceHealthWindows.ts:186-187`); the new `radon-mcp.service` is a
non-timer daemon covered by the units glob for crash shapes only — its liveness gap is R-554.

**Regression review of REL-168…REL-180 (#212) and the testing squash (#213), by EXECUTION.** Five fixes
HOLD with gap probes executed (REL-168 body-stall: concurrent `/healthz` 200, stalled conn closed at 1.0s;
REL-170 private-peer data reads all 401; REL-174 403 classified `auth` with one POST; REL-179 40 passed;
REL-169 8+31 passed). Four are PARTIAL, each with literal executed evidence, filed as R-565…R-568:
REL-180's redactor passes a tail-boundary fragment; REL-171's `BadStatusLine` escapes unstructured;
REL-172's verb cooldown dies with the daemon process; REL-173's double-fault loses the rotated token
without disabling. The testing squash changed four non-test files, all tightening; no guard weakened.
The lead separately EXECUTED the ATS claim (R-528): the `/tmp/atexit_test.py` repro prints 'exiting now'
then hangs until killed — exit 124 on python3.13.

**Severity decisions in the lead.** Every P1 was verified by reading or executing before it landed
(R-521 bootstrap structure, R-523 `quote()`, R-524 `writeFile`, R-525 `isSetupMode`, R-526 zero
`request_body` in `cloud/caddy/`, R-527 route line 63, R-529 cap/reset/no-callers/no-listeners greps,

control-plane walks and merged before numbering (R-560); the preferences clobber was reached by the setup
and control-plane walks from two different files and filed as ONE finding (R-548) because a fix on either
side alone leaves the defect live; the MCP health invisibility was reached by the MCP walk and lead
sweep 7 (R-554).

**The week's theme is new surfaces shipped without their failure half.** The profile overhaul added a
writer of production secrets with no atomicity, dotenv-unsafe quoting, and a derived (unlatched) setup
mode — five of eleven P1s are in that one commit (b0322b6f). The hosted MCP is the first new
internet-facing process in months and shipped with unbounded request/response bodies and the full
production env. The loops' own dead-man contract is split-brained across five wrappers (three still
equate exit-0 with OK). And two of the range's three incident FIXES do not survive scrutiny: the ATS
time-bound provably hangs (R-528, executed) and the docs-gate repair left its fail-open core (R-560).
NF-3 recurs at its widest (R-529), NF-9 recurs for the sixth consecutive new timer (R-555), NF-10 recurs
twice (R-558, R-559).

### Findings

| ID | Sev | Where | Finding |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-519 | P1 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:533-534`; `scripts/security_nightly.sh:555-556`; same shape in the other three wrappers; `.github/workflows/ci.yml:904-910`; commit `32318ae4` | **Every nightly loop executes the untested tip of main.** `reground_for_continuation` does `git checkout -f main && git reset --hard origin/main` with no check that CI is green for that SHA, and main carries no required status checks (the deploy job's own comment concedes it). The 32318ae4 incident already went through exactly this door: three individually-green same-day merges collided semantically and the merged combination 'was again handing the notifier the whole shared .env — IB_FLEX_TOKEN, TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN, CLERK_*' until the 09:29 repair, a window in which the 00:00-00:40 loops would have run the leaking combination. The repair fixed the instance and added no gate against the next one. Remedy shape: resolve the last CI-green main SHA (the `gh api .../runs` lookback already exists in `scripts/ci/path_filter.py`) and reset to that instead of tip. Walk: control plane. |
| R-520 | P1 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:716-732`; contrast `scripts/security_nightly.sh:318`, `:724-728` and `scripts/testing_weekend.sh:325-334`, `:690` | **Three of five loops still equate exit-0 with OK, so the 2026-08-31 false-OK shape recurs unguarded.** security got a completion marker (9c57785f) and testing got a commit check (#213), but reliability, documentation and ci-performance kept `phase_status` = 'rc 0 + no ceiling marker means OK'. The documented failure — agent answers a nudge with text, commits nothing, exits 0, every dead-man channel says OK for an 18-minute night — pages OK in those three loops today. An audit that files nothing against a real delta reads as a clean night and the backlog silently ages. Walk: wrappers. |
| R-521 | P1 | `scripts/api/routes/credentials.py:282-293`; `scripts/api/server.py:756`; contrast `scripts/secret_store.py:262` | **One corrupted credential row crashes radon-api at boot, and keeps crashing it under `Restart=always`.** `bootstrap_exported_names` guards only the listing: `store = _store()` sits before the `try` (raises `SecretStoreError` on a wrong-size key file) and the export loop's `store.get_secret(name)` sits after the `except Exception` that the comment says exists so 'startup must not die on this'. `get_secret` raises `SecretIntegrityError` on any row failing GCM authentication, and the lifespan call is unwrapped. One tampered/corrupted row in `~/.radon/secrets.db` and the IB control plane, order funnel and `/health` never come up until the row is hand-deleted. `list_secrets` proves the safe per-row pattern exists (`except InvalidTag`); it is not used on the boot path. Verified in the lead by reading the path. Walk: credentials. |
| R-522 | P1 | `scripts/secret_store.py:108-137`; `scripts/api/routes/credentials.py:57-59` | **A missing key file silently mints a new key, permanently orphaning every stored credential.** `_load_or_create_key` falls through to `key = os.urandom(_KEY_BYTES)` + O_EXCL create whenever `self._key_path.is_file()` is false, and the store is constructed fresh per request (`def _store(): return SecretStore()`). A deleted/unreadable key file (disk restore, `~/.radon` cleanup, HOME change under systemd) means the next PUT writes new ciphertext under a brand-new key while every existing row becomes undecryptable — no warning, no sentinel binding DB to key; the failure surfaces later as the R-521 boot crash. Same mechanic splits systemd (`CREDENTIALS_DIRECTORY`) from CLI (file key): each writes rows the other reads as tampered. Two processes racing first-use: the O_EXCL loser raises raw `FileExistsError`, not `SecretStoreError` — a 500. Walk: credentials. |
| R-523 | P1 | `web/lib/setup/envFiles.ts:33-35` vs the file's own header `:4-9` | **The setup wizard quotes secrets for a shell, but the declared consumers are dotenv parsers — a credential containing a single quote is silently truncated.** `quote()` emits the POSIX `'\''` idiom, yet the header names the consumers as `@next/env`/dotenv (web/.env) and python-dotenv (root .env), both of which close a single-quoted value at the first embedded quote. An operator pastes a password like `RX$ab'cd` (the repo's documented incident class is special-character passwords): shell sourcing reads it fine, dotenv loads `RX$ab` — and the leftover fragment desyncs the parser for the following line, which for a crafted value can parse as a NEW KEY=VALUE assignment (env-var injection into web/.env). No test covers a value containing a quote; the only incident-derived protection (single-quoting for `$`) is present but incomplete. Verified in the lead. Walk: setup. |
| R-524 | P1 | `web/lib/setup/envFiles.ts:63-74`, `:80-99`; `web/app/api/setup/complete/route.ts:127`; contrast `scripts/utils/atomic_io.py` | **The wizard truncate-writes the root `.env` that already holds operator secrets — a crash mid-write destroys them all.** `upsertEnvFile` does a plain `readFile` then `fs.writeFile(filePath, content, {mode: 0o600})`; `writeFile` opens O_TRUNC, so the existing file (MenthorQ creds, Clerk keys, archive keys on a configured host) is zeroed before the new bytes land, with no temp+rename (the repo's own `atomic_io.py` convention) and no backup. Two concurrent completes (two tabs, both holding the token — the button guard is per-tab state) interleave read-modify-write and one tab's keys are silently lost; and `writeSetupEnvFiles` writes root `.env` then `web/.env` with no rollback, so a failure on the second write 500s AFTER FastAPI has stored and live-exported the credentials — root configured, web/.env without Clerk keys, the stack re-entering setup mode half-materialized. Verified in the lead. Walk: setup. |
| R-525 | P1 | `web/lib/setup/setupMode.ts:14-19`; `web/middleware.ts:436-437`; `web/lib/setup/setupToken.ts:17`, `:21-28`; `web/app/api/setup/complete/route.ts:88-93` | **Setup mode is derived, not latched: any Clerk-env loss on the production host re-opens the setup surface and 503s the whole app.** `isSetupMode()` is merely 'both Clerk env vars empty right now' — no first-run marker — and `handleSetupModeGate` runs BEFORE Clerk and before the probe bearer gate. The repo documents the exact production failure that blanks env vars silently (unquoted `$` under `set -a` sourcing). A `radon-nextjs` restart after such a failure instantly drops the auth wall, 503s every API including probes (total outage), and exposes `POST /api/setup/complete` — which overwrites the production `.env` and proxies credential PUTs into the live FastAPI store — to anyone presenting the setup token, which sits in journald because `setupToken.ts` prints it to stdout; a persistent `RADON_SETUP_TOKEN` override makes the replay window permanent. Verified in the lead (`return !(publishableKey \|\| '').trim() && !(secretKey \|\| '').trim()`). Walk: setup. |
| R-526 | P1 | `cloud/caddy/Caddyfile:75-83`; `scripts/mcp_hosted/server.py:227-246`; `cloud/services/radon-mcp.service:10-11`, `:24`, `:26-27`; `scripts/watchdog/units.py:7-8` | **An anonymous POST body can OOM the hosted MCP and park its unit `failed` permanently.** Nothing bounds the inbound body anywhere on the path: the `handle /mcp*` block has no `request_body max_size` (zero occurrences in `cloud/caddy/`, lead-verified), Caddy's default is unlimited, and FastMCP's stateless streamable-HTTP transport (`stateless_http=True, json_response=True`) reads the full body into memory before parsing. `MemoryMax=512M` turns a multi-hundred-MB anonymous POST into an OOM kill; `RestartSec=2` + `StartLimitIntervalSec=60`/`StartLimitBurst=5` means five such requests in a minute park the unit as `start-limit-hit`, which by design never auto-recovers. The units watchdog pages P1, but detection is not mitigation. P1 not P0: the trading cascade is unaffected (the unit deliberately has no `Requires=`). Walk: MCP. |
| R-527 | P1 | `web/app/api/ma-ratio/route.ts:27-30`, `:56-70`; contrast `web/lib/dbFirstRead.ts:70-90` (`staleCollapse`) | **`/api/ma-ratio` serves arbitrarily stale data as current; `MA_RATIO_MAX_AGE_MS` is dead code — the R-125 class recurring in a new route.** The route's comment says a snapshot older than 48h 'means the writer is down', passes `maxAgeMs` into `dbFirstRead`, then serves `result.ok ? result.data : MISSING_MA_RATIO` — never reading `result.fresh` and never importing `staleCollapse`, the helper that exists precisely to serve `missing + stale + scan_time` (lead-verified: `:63` is the only consumer line). If the timer dies, three weeks later the route still returns the last payload with no stale marker under 300s/3600s cache headers; the desktop panel is partially saved by FreshnessRail, but the assistant `read` pin and any signal derivation off `maRatioZoneTurnUp` receive dead data indistinguishable from live. Walk: ma-ratio. |
| R-528 | P1 | `scripts/fetch_equibles_ats_venue_share.py:619-632` (7e1a5b63); contrast `scripts/fetch_equibles_filing_forensics.py:573-586` (cc53c467) | **The ATS 'stop before TimeoutStartSec' fix does not work — CONFIRMED by execution in the lead.** `_fetch_ticker_bounded` uses `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)` and on timeout `executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)`, but CPython registers `threading._register_atexit(_python_exit)` which joins every worker; a tarpitted worker blocks interpreter exit indefinitely. Lead repro on python3.13: a script that submits a hung worker, shuts down without waiting, prints 'exiting now' — and then hangs until killed (exit 124). The sibling filings commit, 44 minutes later, states this was empirically confirmed and switched to a raw `daemon=True` thread ('verified on 3.13: abandoned executors re-page Result=timeout'); it was never back-ported to ATS. Next Equibles tarpit: ATS writes a `state=error` health row, 'exits cleanly', then hangs in `threading._shutdown` until systemd's `TimeoutStartSec=900` SIGTERM — the same non-latching re-page the commit claims to stop, with a health row contradicting the unit state. Walk: fetchers; lead-executed. |
| R-529 | P1 | `web/lib/usePrices.ts:700-703`, `:815-867`, `:667-680`; `web/lib/reconnectStrategy.ts:23`; `web/components/Providers.tsx:32-38` (0f7e66bf) | **Reconnect exhaustion is now permanent for the life of the tab, and frozen prices survive every navigation — NF-3 at its widest.** `scheduleReconnect` gives up after `maxAttempts ?? 10` (backoff sum ~181s); the strategy resets only in `ws.onopen`, there is no `visibilitychange`/`online` listener (lead-verified: zero occurrences), and nothing outside tests calls the exposed `reconnect()` (lead-verified). Before 0f7e66bf the cap was harmless — every navigation remounted `usePrices` with a fresh strategy; now the ONE instance lives in root `Providers` for the tab's life, publishing a new page's symbols while closed never reconnects (`:861-867` requires `connStateRef === 'open'`), and `prices` is never cleared on close, so every entry keeps rendering. One ~3.2-minute relay outage (crash loop, botched deploy) and every open tab silently serves last-known marks — position cards, chain, modify-modal quotes — all day, until a manual hard reload. The provider's own comment ('Reconnect for genuine drops is unchanged') is true per-mount, but the mount lifetime changed from page to tab. Walk: realtime. |
| R-530 | P2 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:573-576`, `:636-644`; same shape in all five wrappers | **The quota-exhaustion detector reads the agent's own transcript, so a crashing round that QUOTES the patterns is misclassified as quota exhaustion.** `is_quota_exhausted` greps the round slice of the claude `--output-format text` log for 'out of usage credits'/limit/429/529 phrases, and runs before the transient-network retry. These loops audit these very wrappers: a finding about the ladder, a diff of the detector, an RCA mentioning '529 Overloaded' in the final message plus any non-zero exit drops a rung; repeated, the ladder walks to `ALL_MODELS_EXHAUSTED` and the night is reported 'top up at claude.ai' while a real crash is masked. Filed P2, not the walk's P1: per the R-503 precedent the blast radius is the loops' own output and the runner host, not a production path — the row records the disagreement. (R-426 covers only slice scoping.) Walk: wrappers. |
| R-531 | P2 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:340-347`, `:275-288`, `:160-169`, `:211-221`; `config/com.radon.*-daily.plist` (no `ExitTimeOut`); all five wrappers | **The SIGTERM dead-man performs up to five sequential 120s-bounded gh calls before the 10s Pushover curl, inside launchd's ~20s default ExitTimeOut.** `on_signal` runs `gh issue list` (and possibly `create` + a second `list`), `gh issue comment`, then `notify_phase` (`gh pr list` then curl), and only afterwards `release_runner_lock`. On the flaky-VPN nights these bounds were written for, one hung gh call eats the grace window: SIGKILL lands before any channel fires and before the lock release — the observable R-384 was fixed to eliminate, as a residual of that fix. The only GitHub-independent channel (curl, max-time 10) is sequenced LAST. Filed P2 (runner blast radius, R-503 precedent; the walk said P1). Walk: wrappers. |
| R-532 | P2 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:624-627`; `scripts/security_nightly.sh:669-672`; `scripts/testing_weekend.sh:650-653`; `scripts/documentation_nightly.sh:627-630`; `scripts/ci_performance_nightly.sh:631-634` | **Orphan reaping after a normally-exited round is dead code: `ROUND_PID` is cleared before `kill_round_group` is called, and `kill_round_group` returns immediately on empty.** `timeout` only signals the group on cap expiry; a claude that crashes before the cap leaving subagent children is never reaped, and the next round's `git clean -fdxq` races their writes — the exact R-386 race the comment beside the call says the group kill exists to prevent. Either the call precedes the clear, or the claim is false. Walk: wrappers. |
| R-533 | P2 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:568`, `:690`, `:720-722`; siblings in all five wrappers | **`ALL_MODELS_EXHAUSTED` is never reset per phase, so in `cycle` mode an audit-phase exhaustion mislabels a subsequently successful remediate.** `begin_phase` resets `RC` but not the flag (rung carry is intended; flag carry is not). Quota refills mid-cycle: a remediate that exits 0 is still reported 'FAILED (all model quotas exhausted)' in reliability, and in security is forced to RC=75 + 'the next fire resumes the same private run' after a phase that printed its completion marker and did advance state. Walk: wrappers. |
| R-534 | P2 | `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:275-288`; identical in all five wrappers | **`report()` swallows every gh failure invisibly, so a gh auth expiry on the runner reproduces the 'runner did not fire' observable on all five issues at once while Pushover still says OK.** Every `gh issue list/create/comment` is `2>/dev/null \|\| true` with no stderr line and no `$RUN_LOG` line on failure. The dead-man contract ('a missing daily comment means the runner did not fire') has a second, silent producer; nothing records that the comment was attempted and failed. One tee'd log line disambiguates. (R-508 covers the Pushover-side ambiguity only.) Walk: wrappers. |

A final message in the 2026-08-31 parked-mid-flight shape that RECITES its contract ('I am not printing SECURITY-NIGHTLY PHASE COMPLETE: because the scan is still running') contains the marker and flips INCOMPLETE to OK — re-opening the hole 9c57785f closed. Anchor to the slice's last non-empty line. Walk: wrappers. |

Same class: the settings scan covers `$HOME/.claude/settings.json` and the clone's files but not `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json` (absent today; a reroute placed there passes all checks). Walk: wrappers. |
| R-537 | P2 | `scripts/testing_weekend.sh:690`; contrast `scripts/security_nightly.sh:724-729` | **testing's INCOMPLETE leaves RC=0, so an unfinished night exits success — the same condition exits 75 in security.** `status="$INCOMPLETE_STATUS"` assigns no RC; the cycle exits `RC_REMEDIATE=0`. Anything keyed to the exit code (launchd cycle log, contract tests, future orchestration) reads a no-commit testing night as green. Inconsistency introduced by the same delta that added both detectors. Walk: wrappers. |
| R-538 | P2 | `scripts/api/routes/credentials.py:159-172`, `:201`, `:221` | **Rotating one field of a multi-field service 500s if a sibling row is corrupted.** `_merged_values` calls `store.get_secret(field.name)` for every field not in the submission, and the PUT's `try/except SecretStoreError` wraps only `_save`. A corrupted `MENTHORQ_USER` row makes every attempt to rotate `MENTHORQ_PASS` an uncaught `SecretIntegrityError` 500; the escape (DELETE then re-add) is undiscoverable from the UI. Walk: credentials. |
| R-539 | P2 | `scripts/api/routes/credentials.py:216-231`; `scripts/secret_store.py` (no sqlite exception conversion anywhere) | **Multi-field saves are not atomic, and real store I/O failures miss the crafted 503 path entirely.** `_save` loops `set_secret` + `os.environ` export per field — a failure on field 2 leaves field 1 stored AND exported while the response claims the store is unavailable. Worse, `secret_store.py` never converts sqlite exceptions: disk-full, `database is locked` (two concurrent PUTs; no WAL/busy-timeout), read-only-FS all surface as raw `sqlite3.OperationalError`, which is not a `SecretStoreError` — the `CREDENTIAL_STORE_UNAVAILABLE` 503 handler is dead code for exactly the I/O failures its docstring claims to cover. Walk: credentials. |
| R-540 | P2 | `scripts/credentials_registry.py:171-183`; `docker/ib-gateway/docker-compose.yml:8-9`; `scripts/api/ib_gateway.py:650-660` | **IB Gateway password rotation via the profile UI is a false control surface.** The registry note promises `TWS_PASSWORD` is 'Verified by the Gateway itself on next start', but nothing consumes the stored value: docker mode reads `/run/secrets/ib_password` (`TWS_PASSWORD_FILE`), cloud mode's helper owns its own `EnvironmentFile`; only `TWS_USERID` interpolates from the environ. An operator rotating the IBKR password during a real credential rotation gets a gateway restarting with the OLD password — repeated failed logins against a live account at exactly the moment credentials are in flux. The pattern the preferences contract forbids (a control whose described effect is false). Walk: credentials. |
| R-541 | P2 | `scripts/api/routes/credentials.py:122`, `:258-271`; `web/components/profile/CredentialsPanel.tsx:46-50` | **Post-delete provenance lie: 'Using the value from .env' for a value that came from the deleted row.** DELETE removes the row but deliberately leaves the exported `os.environ` value; `env_fallback` is `row is None and bool(os.environ.get(...))`, so immediately after Clear the UI reports a durable .env fallback that does not exist — the live value is the just-deleted secret and silently vanishes on the next restart. Walk: credentials. |
| R-542 | P2 | `scripts/credential_validators.py:206-208`; `scripts/validate_login.py:100-103`, `:121` | **Validator verdicts carry raw child-output fragments back in responses, crossing the module's own redaction contract.** `_run_login_subprocess` embeds the last 200 chars of child stderr/stdout into the 422 detail and the saved `validation.message`; `validate_login.py` prints arbitrary Playwright exception text (selectors, page snippets) and the invalid-verdict includes the final URL — post-login redirects can carry the submitted email in query params. Operator-only surface, but it is the one place raw child output escapes. Walk: credentials. |
| R-543 | P2 | `scripts/credential_validators.py:173-187` | **The Turso validator posts the auth token to an arbitrary operator-supplied URL — plaintext http accepted, any 2xx = 'valid'.** Only `libsql://` is rewritten to https; an `http://` or arbitrary-host `TURSO_DB_URL` ships `Authorization: Bearer` there, and a 2xx from that host stores the pair as vendor-verified. No scheme/host pinning on the credential that owns the canonical trade journal. Related: PUT/validate have no server-side rate limit (the Exa probe burns a paid search per call). Walk: credentials. |
| R-544 | P2 | `scripts/secret_store.py:120-127`, `:141-148` | **Store permission drift is never re-checked.** The 0600 chmod runs only when the DB did not pre-exist; a restored 0644 `secrets.db`, a root-created file, or a 0644 key file (`read_bytes()` with no mode check) is accepted silently forever. Small window besides: `sqlite3.connect` creates the file at umask default before the chmod, and `-journal` siblings inherit umask. Walk: credentials. |
| R-545 | P2 | `web/lib/setup/setupToken.ts:14-33`; `web/components/setup/SetupWizard.tsx:194-196` | **The 'one-shot' setup token is neither one-shot nor stable.** Nothing consumes or rotates it — `verifySetupToken` succeeds indefinitely, and after a successful complete the endpoints stay live (env writes do not change `process.env`, so `isSetupMode()` stays true until restart), allowing unlimited replay of `/api/setup/complete`. Conversely `let generated` is module-level state: a dev recompile mid-wizard 401s the operator's held token, and separately-bundled route entrypoints can each mint their OWN token. It also prints lazily on first API hit, not at launch as the UI copy claims. Walk: setup. |
| R-546 | P2 | `web/lib/setup/envFiles.ts:43-51`; `web/app/api/setup/complete/route.ts:130-137` | **Duplicate keys in an existing .env make the wizard's write silently ineffective.** Only the FIRST occurrence of a key is rewritten (`if (!remaining.has(key)) return line`); shell sourcing and dotenv both let the LAST assignment win, so on a hand-edited file with `UW_TOKEN=old` twice the wizard reports `written: [...]` while the stale value still loads after restart. Walk: setup. |
| R-547 | P2 | `web/components/setup/SetupWizard.tsx:48`; `web/app/api/setup/complete/route.ts:25`, `:76-123`; `web/app/api/setup/validate/route.ts:17` | **The wizard client aborts at 110s while the server's worst case is ~200s, producing false failures and double-stores.** The route loops services sequentially with `STORE_TIMEOUT_MS=100_000` each and the registry ships two `slow` services (90s real browser logins); the browser's `AbortSignal.timeout(110_000)` fires first, the wizard shows 'setup failed' at the collect step while the server keeps going, stores everything and writes the env files. The operator retries: vendor logins re-run, credentials re-store, restart instructions never shown. Walk: setup. |
| R-548 | P2 | `web/lib/uiPreferences.ts:57-64`, `:78-104`; `web/app/api/profile/route.ts:229-236`; `web/lib/useColumnVisibility.ts:56-73`, `:89-98` | **UI preferences clobber each other from two directions — a hydration race and a save inside a React updater — and every flush is a whole-object REPLACE.** (a) `cache = cache ?? prefs` lets a theme toggle made before the profile GET resolves discard the server copy; the 800ms flush then PUTs the partial object over the row (`ui_preferences = excluded.ui_preferences` replaces wholesale), wiping columns on all devices; a transient 401/503 caches `{}` with the same effect. (b) `saveUiColumns` fires INSIDE the `setVisible` updater — React documents updaters as pure and may re-invoke or discard them, so the server can be written a state the screen never committed; the post-mount hydration builds `merged` from defaults + server, reverting a toggle made in the fetch window. TWO walks converged here (setup, web shell); filed as ONE finding because a fix on either side alone leaves the clobber live. Blast radius is preferences only. Walks: setup + control plane. |
| R-549 | P2 | `web/app/api/setup/complete/route.ts:127`; `web/lib/setup/envFiles.ts:82`; contrast `scripts/dev:83`, `cloud/services/radon-nextjs.service:16` | **`repoRoot` is an unvalidated `cwd/..` assumption aimed at exactly the audience that will violate it.** `path.resolve(process.cwd(), '..')` has no marker probe; the shipped launchers set cwd to `web/` so they are fine — but the wizard's audience is a fresh-clone operator who may run `next dev` from anywhere, in which case secrets are written 0600 into an unrelated parent directory, the route reports success, and restart loops back into setup mode with no diagnostic. Walk: setup. |
| R-550 | P2 | `scripts/mcp_hosted/server.py:95-103` | **`_http_get` is documented 'bounded' but downloads the entire upstream body before checking the bound.** `resp.content[: MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES + 1]` slices after `requests` has buffered the whole response (no `stream=True`); the 2 MiB cap bounds what is returned, not what is allocated. A large operator `/api/journal` payload or a misbehaving upstream buffers fully inside the 512M cgroup — the same park-as-failed ladder as R-526, triggered by upstream data. Fix shape: `stream=True` + `iter_content` with a running count. Walk: MCP. |
| R-551 | P2 | `scripts/mcp_hosted/auth.py:69-118` vs `scripts/api/auth.py:190-256` | **The MCP auth module claims to 'mirror scripts/api/auth.py' but omits every JWKS bounding control the mirror has.** No `MAX_JWKS_INFLIGHT` admission, no per-kid in-flight dedupe, no negative-kid cache, no `asyncio.wait_for` — just a synchronous `get_signing_key_from_jwt` in the anyio threadpool. PyJWKClient force-refreshes on unknown kid, so an anonymous flood of self-signed RS256 tokens with random kids makes one outbound Clerk JWKS fetch per request (30s default timeout each): cheap amplification against Clerk plus pinning of the ~40-thread pool, stalling legitimate operator reads. Walk: MCP. |
| R-552 | P2 | `scripts/mcp_hosted/server.py:166-189`, `:272-288`; `cloud/caddy/Caddyfile:75-83` | **Anonymous tools perform unthrottled outbound fetches; no rate limit exists at any layer.** `radon_docs` and `radon_health` need no credentials and each issues a live GET (15s timeout) to radon.run / the edge-health daemon; Caddy adds no rate limit on `/mcp*`, uvicorn has no `limit_concurrency`, the process no per-caller throttle. Forty concurrent anonymous calls saturate the sync-tool threadpool; sustained load queues in memory, compounding R-526. Fixed URLs, so no SSRF — but a free anonymous lever on a production host. ### Audited, clean

- Order funnel: no new placement site; the delta's order-touching UI posts to the guarded
  `/api/orders/place`; `mcp_hosted` registers no write tool and every proxied path is a fixed read-only
  GET re-authenticated upstream with the caller's own token (auth default-deny verified fail-closed).
- Gate-3 flatten reframing (31c39238): every `closeOut` producer bounds to held size before setting it
  (`positionTrade.ts:302-320`, `BookTab.tsx:366-382`, `MobileOrderTicket.tsx:317-356`, `OrderTab.tsx:856-870`,
  `ModifyOrderModal.tsx:407-490`, `FuturesOrderForm.tsx:133-150`), so an opening/over-sized order cannot be
  framed risk-reducing. Dep arrays in all delta hooks list everything their bodies read.
- `web/public/design.md`/`design-example.html`/`radon.css`: fully synthetic XMPL data, no PII/tokens;
  middleware exemption is one exact path. `cloud/config/installed-units.sha256`: all four changed hashes
  byte-exact. Gitleaks allowlists commit-exact and rule-scoped (`test_gitleaks_policy.py:167-178`).
- ma-ratio: divide-by-zero/empty-universe handling solid (`compute_ratio` None on `pct200 == 0`, coverage
  gates, web null-guards); Turso-first chunked upsert; registered in both catalogs + refreshSchedule +
  regimeRail. Migration 0068, unit and timer clean.
- Wrappers: `github_pr_output.py`, `nightly_issue_format.py`, `weekend_notify.py` (key-scoped env import),
  all five `setup_*` deltas (per-loop venv split sound), plist deltas (bounded pre-lock fetch, staggered
  minutes, PATH resolves).
- FastAPI delta: order-rate reservation/consume, operator-JWT tightening, 504/502 sentinel mapping;
  admin unit/action route canonicalization mirrors FastAPI with correct timeout ordering (150s > 135s);
  `assistant_catalog.py` credentials -> `admin`, fail-closed. 34b37f0a's header/chip classification now
  derives from the same `mapOrderStatus` (the counting granularity residual is R-564).
- Setup/status/validate routes and `web/app/setup/page.tsx`; migration 0067; web credentials routes,
  `profile/route.ts` auth (operatorOnly, no demo path, GET pinned admin, PUT/DELETE rate-limited),
  `credentials.ts`, `ProfileContent.tsx`.
- Realtime: `Providers.tsx` mount ownership, `payoffRatio` (0.995-0.999 rounds up then falls through),
  `sessionWindow`/`orderDisplay` extended-hours promotion bounded to ext-eligible STK/FUT in the two ET
  windows, mobile Card/OrderTicket/PositionList/OrderList (shared price helpers, unconditional hooks).
- Filings fetcher (`fetch_equibles_filing_forensics.py`): daemon-thread bound + unconditional error row on
  `budget_spent` — the correct sibling the ATS fix should copy.

## Delta audit 2026-09-03

**Range:** `db25990d..HEAD` (`0202e32d`) — 17 commits, 68 substantive source files after dropping tests,
docs, `site/` and `context/`. Anchor verified (`git rev-parse --verify db25990d^{commit}` resolves;
`git merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms). Four loop-output squashes (Reliability #243, Testing #240,
Documentation #242, CI-Performance #241) were split from the 12 feature commits by commit identity; the
operator's 6aec2268 (which consumed REL-190/REL-192/REL-204 with no log rows) went to the EXECUTING
regression walk together with the reliability squash's REL-189/REL-191. Five walks capped at 8-13 files
finished in 1.5-3.5 minutes, none lost to the stream watchdog.

**No branch collision.** `git ls-remote --heads origin reliability/2026-09-03` was run BEFORE numbering and
returned nothing. **Numbering starts at R-574:** the max over both documents plus `git log --grep` since the
anchor is R-573/REL-208 (the delta's commit messages consume nothing above). Backlog continues at REL-209.

**Standing sweeps** ran in the lead before the walks reported; sweeps 1-5 hold: halt chokepoint
(`ib_place_order.py`), `_NON_IDEMPOTENT_IB_SCRIPTS` intact (`server.py:5359`, enforced `:5468`), order limits
wired, exit-orders ack-poll present (`handlers/exit_orders.py:182`), hrana on `writer.py:35`. Sweep 6: the
delta's only added `place_order` text is documentation; no new order-placing call site. Sweep 7: the new
`radon-iv-spread.timer` is registered in both catalogs (`watchdog/services.py:251`,
`serviceHealthWindows.ts:222`) with manifest hashes present.

**Regression review of REL-189/REL-191 (#243) and the operator's REL-190/REL-192/REL-204 (6aec2268), by
EXECUTION.** Shipped tests all green (pytest 13+72 passed; vitest 33+11 passed). REL-189's store HOLDS
(one P3 residue, R-595) and REL-190 HOLDS (chmod-000 key self-healed to 0600). FOUR are PARTIAL with
literal executed evidence: REL-189's route half (R-592), REL-191's multiline half (R-593), REL-192's
env-refusal half (R-591), REL-204's columns half (R-594).

**Severity decisions in the lead.** Both P1s were CONFIRMED by lead reads before numbering (R-578's
except-pass-grace-0 path; R-584's unsorted batch + unconditional duplicate-ok). Two walk claims were
REJECTED and recorded in the surviving rows: the iv-spread/ivrank timer overlap (R-577's row — ivrank's
latest fire is 22:12, iv-spread's earliest 22:15) and the fill-monitor alert-window mismatch (R-583's row —
`extended: 3*DAY` already exists). The control-plane walk verified the git-protocol-v1 tip read fail-closed
end to end (bounded, regex-gated, `return 69` before any install) — recorded as CLEAN in R-589's row.

**The week's theme is the missing failure half of otherwise-good fixes.** The extended-hours commit added
the correct gate with a fail-to-never error path (R-578); the heartbeat-ownership move made the duplicate
path unconditionally green (R-584); the share-cap fix widened the exact path that skips the notional check
(R-580); and all four PARTIAL regressions are the untested half of a fix whose tested half holds. NF-9
recurs on the new timer's crash paths only (R-574 — the success and unchanged-data paths are honest);
NF-3 recurs twice in compensated form (R-576, R-585); NF-10 does NOT recur — the one new suppression
(R-583's window) is clock-bounded.

### Findings

|---|---|---|---|
| R-574 | P2 | `scripts/fetch_iv_spread.py:524-527, :590` | **Three iv-spread exit paths write no heartbeat (NF-9 residue).** `_cached_fallback` raises when IB is down and no cached payload exists; `--backfill` with IB unavailable raises at :524; and the post-fetch 'no session is present on both legs' RuntimeError at :590 fires after a successful IB fetch but before `_write_db`. First-ever run on a fresh host with IB in 2FA exits 1 with no error row; only the 26h staleness window eventually notices. Contrast `server.py:1289-1297`, which falls back to RTH on the same error — the docstring's 'a calendar regression cannot silently unsync the book' promise holds only server-side. tzdata or `market_calendar` import breakage on the VPS means no fill detection, no journal mirror, detection only via the fill-monitor row going stale. Pre-existing for options, but bdbe31de widened the qty cap 2500-&gt;10,000 on exactly this path.
