## Delta audit 2026-09-04

Range: `0202e32d..2b936ebc` -- 50 commits. Eight loop-output squashes (the five nightly loops' own
merges plus `a6ba33c1`, `8e430acb`, `dd81b66c`) were split off by commit identity, leaving 28
feature commits over 44 source files after filtering tests, docs, `site/` and `context/`.
Method: seven parallel read-only walks capped at 4-12 files each (nightly-loop wrappers, money path / reconciliation / ingestion, watchdog / health / observability, control plane / deploy / host, auth / credentials / secret store, web serving / order display / assistant, and one EXECUTING regression walk over the 2026-09-03 remediation),
plus the seven standing sweeps run in the LEAD context. `git ls-remote --heads origin
'reliability/*' ` was run BEFORE numbering: no branch existed. Anchor `0202e32d` verified
(`rev-parse --verify` resolves to `0202e32d54949b7dd640b2ad630b60b4a7160723`,
`merge-base --is-ancestor` confirms). Highest consumed id across BOTH documents and
`git log --grep` since the anchor is R-595 / REL-223, so numbering continues at R-596 / REL-224.


**Standing sweeps: all seven HOLD.** Halt chokepoints present at all seven order sites;
`_NON_IDEMPOTENT_IB_SCRIPTS` intact; `order_limits` wired at every placement funnel; the
exit-orders ack poll present; hrana in use. Sweep 6 over the WHOLE repo found no order-placing
call site bypassing both guards -- `scripts/workflow/nodes.py:106` routes through
`ib_place_order.place_order`, which is guarded at `:240`/`:253`. Sweep 7: both catalog-parity
suites green (13 + 25 passed) over 59 timer-backed services with 6 exemptions, and every
exemption reason was re-verified against the resolver in the lead -- all six are honest
(four `parser:` cases resolve to `set()`; the two `wrapper:` cases name rows that ARE in both
catalogs). **NF-8 stays CLOSED for the second consecutive audit.**


**Severity raised from the walk's rating in two rows, with the reasoning in the row:**
`R-603` (the edge dead-man's witness suppression is unbounded and the mechanism it defers to
cannot detect the failure it hides) and `R-610` (a truncated orders read whole-replaces the book
that drives modify and cancel, which is the same outcome the guard was written for and is
strictly more likely than the empty read it does cover).


**Standing classes this delta.** NF-10 (a suppression added to stop a false page) is the
dominant shape again, recurring six times: the Flex cross-query mask, the equibles seven-day
embargo on non-cadence failures, the external-probe green-run witness, the JWKS negative cache,
the assistant error blackhole and the setup token's unbounded lifetime. NF-3 recurs twice (the
Flex duplicate veto, the setup registry fallback). NF-9 recurs twice (the equibles abort path,
and `radon-api.service` never starting at all). **The dominant NEW class is a fix that reopened
its own finding**: four of the thirteen P1s are defects in the 2026-09-03 remediation proved by
execution, and two more are defects in R-389's fix from 2026-08-29.


### Findings

| ID | Sev | Where | Finding |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-596 | P0 | `scripts/nightly_issue_prune.py:35-47`, `:50-62`, `:112-118`; called from `scripts/reliability_weekend.sh:286` and the same line in all four sibling loops | **The dead-man prune fails OPEN into a destructive delete: 'gh is broken' is encoded identically to 'this loop has no open PR'.** `_run()` returns `''` on ANY failure (non-zero rc, timeout, OSError). `open_pr_head_refs` turns that into `[]`, `has_open_pr([])` is `False`, and `main()` falls straight through to `issue_comment_ids` + `delete_comment` for every comment. **Standing sweeps: all seven HOLD.** Halt chokepoints present (`ib_place_order` + server);
`_NON_IDEMPOTENT_IB_SCRIPTS` intact; order-limits wired at every funnel including
`clients/ib_client.py`; exit-order ack present; daemon_state on the hrana transport
(`db/writer.py:35`); the modify-path guards survived the 202 commit (verified in the lead at
`ib_order_manage.py:198-206`, `:282-302`); both catalog-parity suites green (`49 passed`) and
the delta's only timer change (`radon-portfolio-sync` widened to the extended session) is an
already-catalogued service. NF-8 stays CLOSED for the third consecutive audit.

**Executing regression walk of PR #273:** REL-227, REL-230 and REL-187/188 HOLD with literal
output; REL-229 is PARTIAL as a P1 (the fix landed in `mcp_hosted` only -- R-637, executed
401 + 30s kid poison on the FastAPI path), REL-225 PARTIAL as a P2 (R-644, executed 181-poll
3h burn via rc-nonzero/empty-stdout), REL-224 PARTIAL as a P3 (R-657, executed
`pruned 1/1` of today's own post). The "name one case the shipped test does not cover and RUN
it" instruction hit 3-for-6 this cycle.

**Cross-walk convergence and one arbitration.** The 202-cancel defect was reached independently
by the web and python money-path walks (merged into R-634/R-638). The app-role health
suppression drew OPPOSITE verdicts -- the control-plane walk filed it as unbounded NF-10, the
python walk verified the nested `radon-api` broker probe covers the true app-host case -- both
readings are in R-650's row and the severity (P2, not P1) follows from the verified coverage.
The dominant new-defect class this delta is the **client-side race against a live-updating
order prop** (R-633, R-640, R-642): the fills-into-UI work shipped correct data flow with no
concurrency discipline. NF-3 recurs (R-641, the standing stale-feed order-UI gate, now load-
bearing under event-driven fills); NF-10 recurs twice (R-650, R-655); NF-9 shape once (R-655).

**Findings (34: 5 P1, 19 P2, 10 P3, 0 P0):**

| ID | Sev | Where | Finding |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-633 | P1 | `web/components/ModifyOrderModal.tsx:320-334` (seed effect keyed on `orderPermId` only), `:517-530` (`currentQuantity = remainingQuantity(order)` from the live polled prop), `:619` (`toIbTotalQuantity(order, parsedQuantity!)` with live `filled`), `:600-612` (combo replace sends the stale remainder as a new order); `web/app/api/orders/modify/route.ts:198-226` | **A fill landing while the modify dialog is open silently ENLARGES an untouched partial order.** The quantity field is seeded once per permId with the remainder, but `quantityChanged` and the transmitted total are computed against the LIVE order prop on every render. BUY 1000 filled 16: dialog opens at 984; a 100-lot tranche fills mid-edit; a price-only submit now sees 884 != 984, flips `quantityChanged`, and transmits `newQuantity = 116 + 984 = 1100` — the order GROWS on an edit the operator never made. The combo-replace branch has the same race and over-orders: the cancel removes the true (smaller) remainder and the placement puts the stale larger remainder into the market. The commit's stated invariant ('an untouched partial order still submits nothing') holds only while no fill lands during the dialog. Lead-verified at all four sites. AC: fault-injection test that advances the order prop's `filled` between dialog open and submit and asserts (a) a price-only edit transmits NO quantity change and (b) the replace path refuses or re-prompts when live `filled` moved past the open-time snapshot. Walk: web money path (lead-verified). |
| R-634 | P1 | `scripts/ib_order_manage.py:123` (`if reqId == trade.order.orderId or reqId == -1`), `:162`, `:170` (finish `ok`/`Cancelled` on `any(code == 202 ...)` with no state re-read) | **A broadcast IB 202 (reqId=-1) confirms a cancel that may not have happened, and the replacement is placed on top of a working order.** `cancel_order` collects errors for ANY reqId==-1 event; a 202 for a DIFFERENT order on the same clientId (cancel-all, bracket child auto-cancel, a second replace in flight) satisfies `any(code == 202)` and the function exits `ok, finalStatus=Cancelled` while the order is still Submitted — the commit's own `test_cancel_succeeds_on_broadcast_202` pins exactly this. `/orders/replace` (`scripts/api/server.py:3170-3186`) then records it cancelled and places the replacement: doubled live exposure with the UI showing one order. The post-loop check also never consults the refreshed trade snapshot, so a t=0 202 wins over a t=5s `Submitted`. Pre-`4c34bc02` this failed closed. Lead-verified at `:123`/`:162`/`:170`. AC: emit 202 with reqId=-1 while the target order's refreshed status stays `Submitted`; assert `cancel_order` does NOT report Cancelled (matching-reqId 202 or a gone/Cancelled refresh remain confirmations). Walk: python money path (lead-verified). |
| R-635 | P1 | `cloud/scripts/deploy-root-helper.sh:1411-1431` (`compose_body_is_valid`), duplicated at `cloud/scripts/bootstrap-control-plane.sh:344-355` | **The entire content gate on the compose body root executes is four greps, and the host-bind grep matches only the literal `- /:`.** The file it guards, `/etc/radon/ib-gateway-compose.yml`, is handed to `docker compose up -d` as root by `radon-docker-gw`. The validator misses `- "/:/host"` (quoted), `- /etc:/etc`, `- /var/run/docker.sock:...`, long-form `volumes: [{type: bind, source: /}]`, `pid: host`, `network_mode: host`, `cap_add`, `user: root`, `devices:`, `security_opt`. Any body with `- /etc:/host-etc` plus the pinned container_name passes both copies; the shim's 'no caller-supplied mounts' premise rests entirely on git-blob provenance, so this validator is false assurance, and the rule lives in two hand-copied places that can diverge. Lead-verified by reading the function. AC: feed bodies with a quoted root bind, a docker.sock bind, `pid: host` and long-form bind mounts; assert each is refused; assert the two copies are one sourced implementation or a parity test pins them identical. Walk: control plane (lead-verified). |
| R-636 | P1 | `cloud/scripts/setup-vps.sh:1132` (`compose_source="${CLOUD_DIR}/docker-compose.yml"`), `:1158-1166` (`stage_from_checkout` with no validation) | **`setup-vps.sh` installs the compose body from the radon-writable checkout with NO validation and NO provenance — the exact escalation `f5634a01` closed for refresh, left open on the provisioning path.** `install_docker_gw` stages `${CLOUD_DIR}/docker-compose.yml` (production: `/home/radon/radon/cloud`, radon-writable) straight to root-owned `/etc/radon/ib-gateway-compose.yml` with neither `compose_body_is_valid` nor a git-blob read — unlike `deploy-root-helper.sh:1344` and `bootstrap-control-plane.sh:346`. Anything running as radon writes a host-bind compose body into the checkout and waits for the operator's next routine `setup-vps.sh` convergence run (the same commit's own stated remediation path for stripping group docker); root then installs those bytes at the path the shim executes as root. Lead-verified at both cites. AC: test that `install_docker_gw` refuses a body failing the (strengthened, R-635) validator and sources the body via git-blob provenance like the refresh path. Walk: control plane (lead-verified). |
| R-637 | P1 | `scripts/api/auth.py:219` (`except (asyncio.CancelledError, Exception): _remember_negative_kid(kid)`), `:253` (bare `except Exception` -> negative cache + 401); `scripts/mcp_hosted/auth.py:140` (`_is_upstream_outage` vs `PyJWKClientConnectionError`) | **The REL-229 fix landed only in `scripts/mcp_hosted/auth.py`; the FastAPI dashboard path the log row names still negative-caches a Clerk outage and 401s every valid token for 30s.** EXECUTED by the regression walk: an OSError outage produced `401` and poisoned `kid-x` for 30s, with the second call served 401 from the cache — the exact defect R-621/R-630 described, alive in the other module. Secondary, verified by execution: `jwt.PyJWKClientConnectionError` subclasses `PyJWTError`, so mcp_hosted's `_is_upstream_outage` classifies a wrapped connection failure as a token verdict and the fixed module re-opens the same hole through that one exception type. AC: in `scripts/api/auth.py`, a timeout/OSError/5xx during JWKS fetch yields 503 and leaves the kid re-probeable (only PyJWT signature/claim errors populate the negative cache); in mcp_hosted, `PyJWKClientConnectionError` classifies as outage. Red today in both. Walk: executing regression (executed). |
| R-638 | P2 | `scripts/api/server.py:3126-3157` (`_cancel_already_confirmed`), `:3145` (structured 202 short-circuits before the filled check), `:3178-3182` (cancelled entry fabricated from the request) | **The API-side 202 classification is substring matching over free text, and a structured 202 outranks the already-filled guard.** `'order canceled'` is matched anywhere in the stringified detail (including nested `upstream`), with `'already filled'` the only negative guard, so a wrapped failure message that embeds IB's 202 text is promoted to Cancelled and the replacement is placed over a still-working order. The `ib_error_code == 202` branch returns before the filled check, so a 202 arriving alongside an execution on a partial fill still records Cancelled and places the replacement at full quantity. There is no broker-state verification between 'cancel confirmed' and 'place replacement'; the cancelled entry is synthesized from the request. AC: structured detail with `ib_error_code=202` plus a partial-fill execution -> replace refuses or resizes; a 5xx whose text embeds 'Order Canceled' -> NOT confirmed. Walk: web + python money paths (converged). |
| R-639 | P2 | `web/app/api/orders/modify/route.ts:160-166`, `:307`; FastAPI accepts the total verbatim | **No server-side floor on `newQuantity` against the already-filled count.** The route validates id presence and confirms post-hoc via `isModifyConfirmed`, but nothing rejects a total below `filled`; the client snapshot is the only guard, and R-633 shows that snapshot can be stale. A stale shrink is transmitted and IB treats it as fill-and-cancel — silent size reduction the operator did not ask for. AC: modify request with `newQuantity < filled` per the server's own refreshed order snapshot is refused 422 at the wire (full-URL request assertion per the gated-action rule). Walk: web money path. |
| R-640 | P2 | `web/lib/useSyncHook.ts:131` (`if (inFlightRef.current.has(method)) return;`), `web/lib/useFillToasts.ts:89` (exec key added to `seenRef` before the refresh fires), `:95` | **The fill-driven positions refresh is silently dropped when a sync is already in flight, reopening the defect `aad3e06b` fixed.** A fill landing during any in-flight POST sync (60s tick, Sync Now, auto-sync-on-stale) returns early with no queue, no retry, no telemetry; the exec id is already in `seenRef`, so the refresh is never re-attempted for that fill. FILLED toast on screen, positions table pre-fill for up to a full producer period — under concurrency, which is exactly when fills cluster. AC: fire a fill event while a POST is in flight; assert a refresh is re-fired in the request's `finally` (pending flag), not dropped. Walk: realtime serving. |
| R-641 | P2 | `web/components/WorkspaceShell.tsx:439-446` (`liveDataError` from fetch errors only), `:556` (`pricesForSections` feeds orders/portfolio/ticker-detail), `:331-343` (`ibConnected` debounced, banner-only); `web/lib/quoteTelemetry.ts` computes age but gates nothing | **Standing NF-3, unmet on the delta's own surface: no staleness gate on order-relevant marks.** A half-open relay socket or dropped IB market-data line keeps `usePrices` returning the last tick; the shell keeps painting those marks into the order ticket and modify modal and nothing disables submit. Connectivity state drives banners only. The delta wired fills INTO this surface (aad3e06b) without wiring the stale-feed gate the standing finding requires. Walk: realtime serving (NF-3 recurrence). |
| R-642 | P2 | `web/lib/fillToasts.ts:123-128` (`fillGroupKey` prefers recycled `orderId` over `permId`, contradicting its own docstring at `:117-122`), `web/lib/useFillToasts.ts:54`, `:83-90` (`groupsRef` never cleared on dismiss) | **Fill-toast accounting survives dismissal and merges across recycled orderIds, so a money toast can read as a fresh larger execution.** Dismiss the 'FILLED BUY 900x' toast at 900/1000; the last 100 lots land and a fresh toast reads 'FILLED BUY 1000x' — cumulative total presented as a new fill. Separately, IB orderIds recycle per session/clientId while permId is the stable one; with a persisted sessionStorage baseline and an unevicted `groupsRef`, an afternoon order reusing a morning orderId merges into the morning running total with wrong quantity and wrong weighted average.
